- **auroc-performance-computation**: Use when comparing novel cell type detection or cell-type classification methods on single-cell RNA-seq query/reference pairs and you need threshold-independent performance metrics such as AUROC, AUPR and FPR95 per method per setting. - **auroc-performance-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking differential expression (or similar binary gene-label) detection methods against simulated data with known ground-truth DE genes, and you need rank-based performance metrics (AUROC, negative Spearman of p-values vs logFC) to compare methods.
